# One-path renewal litmus report, 2026-11-06

Final audited application and Help-asset source: `edba7cbb29dfc7942257e91e91932516a449fbf2`. Machine run 11: not accepted. 62 Yes, 2 No, 7 UNVERIFIED. Human verdict: NOT RUN. Earlier findings and failed qualifications remain as recorded below.

Shared report format: Yes means observed PASS, No means FAIL, and UNVERIFIED means the stated evidence is missing. An inference or a passing test alone never counts as an actor-outcome pass.

Human verdict: NOT RUN
Staff session: none

## Initial failures

- L10: Show renewals for December 2026 returned six IDs including non-renewal 9138, while This batch had five. Working-month/batch phrases returned all 38; excluded notes omitted non-renewal count.
- L28: After successful creation, unchanged message immediately showed older-draft warning (draft revision669083 vs message020892). First confirmation had refused stale preview despite no edit during review; re-preview succeeded.
- L36: Replied fixture showed date/decision controls but actual reply body was absent (explicit full-text search false). Outcome+verified approver+Record succeeded in three recording actions and advanced tenant, with none preselected.
- L46: Covered owner step said no owner message needed, but orange primary was Send the owner message and Ready/You decide still described owner send. Strip correctly continued at tenant; old tools retained.
- L60: Ledger contains twenty November4 rows with results. Verified N4-18 (standing agreement still asks owner) was contradicted by misleading owner-send primary observed in L46.
- L65: No deposit/multi-pet totals stayed blank with exact needs; assistance showed no pet total or animal registration input. However Guided legacy readiness said Pet deposit + registration and generic staff activity label still said Pet registration follow-up.
- Additional feedback defect: choosing Housing assistance Yes with blank Program name showed no required marker or recoverable feedback, while saved workspace remained unchanged. No registration-specific animal field was found; L65 refers to legacy generic wording.

## Repairs

None observed on this first snapshot. The parent is implementing repairs in separate source commits; this independent session changed no application source. Later observations will be a separate run, never a rewrite of the first outcome.

## Final observations

Initial observed commit: 9fdf624c86eec2d12c102b6243cd277b9a19fcba. The application and own helper used guardedFetch and guardedConnect with only local browser routing. Automatic comparison was held off at startup and after every synthetic reset; no automatic preparation was called. Manual comparison stand-ins were allowed.

The inner controller exited 0 and emulators stopped. The outer wrapper exited 1 because a root-side focused test changed the shared Vitest cache inode through hardlinks; this remains a failed qualification. Initial shell/bootstrap guard coverage and a normalized socket overload were subsequently found incomplete; no historical no-escape proof is inferred from the application refusal log. The disposable worktree was removed and the browser/helper stopped.

Raw evidence is private: /home/josiah/pmi-kc-work/one-path-run/independent-raw; harness log: /home/josiah/.local/state/pmi-kc-one-path-rehearsal/20261106T180800Z-9fdf624c. No raw screenshot is embedded here. Prior builder results in the required ledger/walkthrough artifact were read only as claims to audit; no verdict below is taken from them.
